## China-LATAM-US

<!-- OneNote page ID: {E0A1CC49-A7F4-03C4-1C9B-A0D21C7657D9}{1}{E1845805134490290339618856254607888248331174} -->

Main driver is China business bypassing US/ China trade control
  - Entity in Mexico selling to Texas
  - Trading company in Mexico or reshipping via LATAM
Selling to LATAM market to a lesser extent
  - On intl' platform such as Temu, Amazon
  - On local platform such as Brazil's
Indicators:
  - Competitor movement
  - Trading number
  - Trading route (logistics)
  - Trade volume data
  - Registered companies
  - Case studies
  - 美墨之間的「近岸外包]
  - [https://www.hk01.com/article/1020406?utm_source=01articlecopy&utm_medium=referral](https://www.hk01.com/article/1020406?utm_source=01articlecopy&utm_medium=referral)
  - Sources
  - AMI
---------------------------------------------
eCom
  1. Brazil is the largest LATAM eCom market for Chinese sellers. Mexico, Chile, Colombia are distant followers
  1. June tariff (20% tax on international purchase <= $50) discouraged Chinese sellers from the large but unfamiliar Brazil market, while some are converting to "local shops". DTC is still nascent
B2B
  1. While China export to LATAM is sizable ($110B to Mexico and $60B to Brazil, 2023), the vast majority is traditional trading served by banks (such as machineries, chemicals, etc.)
Mexico nearshoring
  1. Chinese companies have been building factories in Mexico to bypass U.S. tariff (e.g. this China furniture manufacturer selling to U.S. Costco). China FDI in Mexico surged in 2022.
  1. They may have complex payment needs between U.S., Mexico and China. Our ability to serve and market size needs to be validated.

  1. **Market size**: Mastercard estimated the entire global OTA VCC market is 300B annually. The biggest player process 120B( I guess is Wex?) They are second biggest(Doesn’t disclosed but 30B+ at least)
  1. **Card bin**: They are the issuer of Mastercard, Visa Amex and UnionPay respectively (HK and Singapore Bin). They are not the issuer in US and Europe Market. They work with JP in US market.
  1. **Partnership**: They are welcome to work with us. They currently work with AW in China（1. Card bin 2. Commercial terms 3. Credit 4. Redistribution capability are 4 main factors)  They also believe this is the right strategy in Travel vertical for Payo. They take the same strategy in E-commerce market. Skyee and Lian Lian are using their card
  1. **Competitor**: AW becomes the main competitor in this vertical. They give up to**2.7% in US merchant. 2.1% for Non-US merchant**. Visa wants to penetrate in Travel vertical and did give them great sponsorship incentive. Ctrip also integrating their card. **Sunrate around 300M per month but vol drop due to the FX exchange penalty.**
  1. **Margin-** The OTA vcc business margin is defiantly thinner than E-commence. OTA supplier business margin usually are lower than 1% so they do need VCC rebate to reimburse the consumer.

### iPaylinks

<!-- OneNote page ID: {E0A1CC49-A7F4-03C4-1C9B-A0D21C7657D9}{1}{E185059447023408357961853258456561674985422} -->

主要是提供海外本地客户特供采购国内产品的方案
**中国的****OTA****跨境平台以携程为代表企业，融合了酒店、机票、景点门票、租车等旅游产品。**近期，携程与百度地图又“官宣”上线了境外打车服务，一站式整合了Grab等境外主流打车的平台，为海外用户提供境外租车、接送机、包车、打车约车四大服务，截止目前，已覆盖全球56个国家，886个城市。
![research-ipaylinks](assets/research/research-ipaylinks/asset-001.jpg)
![research-ipaylinks](assets/research/research-ipaylinks/asset-002.jpg)
航司、酒店等供应商越来越碎片化，彼此之间竞争激烈，为了确保销售额，每个供应商往往会**对接上千家分销商**来确保订单量。与此同时，每个分销商也会同时对应数以万计的供应商的旅游产品。以机票为例，保守估计，一家航空公司的分销商（包括OTA，代理人等）大概有上千家。为了降低成本，很多分销商之间会跳过供应商直接相互对接，导致分销渠道交叉复杂多样。无论体量大小，由此引发的一系列问题使许多OTA商户都遭受着困扰。
![research-ipaylinks](assets/research/research-ipaylinks/asset-003.jpg)
From Players:
  - 寻汇
  - Ctrip供应商OTA
Type of players:
  - TMC差旅一个月10万美金. 1.5月账期
  - 个人旅行（头部：天泰-也做B2B）：小的3M,大的10+M. 7天账期
  - OTA平台（Ctrip）
  - VCC provider（寻汇）
  - BSP:The BSP preferred method of payment is by direct debit.(but yes can use credit card)[https://www.iata.org/en/services/finance/bsp/bsplink/](https://www.iata.org/en/services/finance/bsp/bsplink/)
  - Supply side平台（e.g. Injetsy)有自己的支付方式，e.g.银行对公
Insights:
  - 机票better / more volume than hotel
  - Winning opportunity: FX pricing (think about overall packaging)
  - Disadvantage:开发票、专票
  - 0.5-1%返点区别可以靠relationship吃下来
  - Working capital?
  - 资金安全
Value chain might be more fragmented:
  - OTA platform like Ctrip vs OTA
  - BSP/ DSP能够查询实时房量的

## B2B

<!-- OneNote page ID: {E0A1CC49-A7F4-03C4-1C9B-A0D21C7657D9}{1}{E185059447023408357961853258456561674985434} -->

根据艾瑞统计测算，2022年中国跨境出口B2B贸易支付总体可实现市场规模（TAM）为4.9万亿元（即在一般贸易额中的中小微企业业务规模总和），同比增长40%，随着出口B2B贸易小额化、碎片化趋势凸显，预计行业TAM仍将持续保持高速增长；2022年各跨境支付机构实际发生业务规模为3451亿元，同比增长73%，但整体市场渗透率仍然不足10%，市场发展空间巨大。

From <[https://www.icbc-ltd.com/icbc/%E7%BD%91%E4%B8%8A%E7%90%86%E8%B4%A2/%E4%B8%93%E5%AE%B6%E8%A7%86%E7%82%B9/%E9%99%88%E6%A4%8D/%E8%B7%A8%E5%A2%83%E7%94%B5%E5%95%86B2B%E6%94%AF%E4%BB%98%E7%9A%84%E5%8F%8D%E6%B4%97%E9%92%B1%E4%B9%8B%E6%88%98.htm](https://www.icbc-ltd.com/icbc/%E7%BD%91%E4%B8%8A%E7%90%86%E8%B4%A2/%E4%B8%93%E5%AE%B6%E8%A7%86%E7%82%B9/%E9%99%88%E6%A4%8D/%E8%B7%A8%E5%A2%83%E7%94%B5%E5%95%86B2B%E6%94%AF%E4%BB%98%E7%9A%84%E5%8F%8D%E6%B4%97%E9%92%B1%E4%B9%8B%E6%88%98.htm)>
Payoneer hiring post
**B2B Risk Strategist**
20-40K·14薪
  - 上海·上海市
  - 5-10年
  - 硕士
岗位描述
What’s the job? • Develop and maintain risk management policies, guidelines, standards, and processes in B2B payment industry • Analyze risks associated with different decisions and propose the best course of action • Perform data mining, modelling and analysis to properly document and report on risk factors • Forecast, track, and measure key business metrics • Develop risk models to demonstrate and track findings • Research, gather, and review data relevant to ongoing or proposed projects • Conduct scenario analyses to inform business strategy • Improve processes and/or streamline workflows • Identify and communicate risk metrics to decision-makers and make advice to management team to balance and business values Is this you? • 8 years of experience in risk-related field and 3+ years of experience in risk policy management Entrepreneurial spirit you will need to think and function like an entrepreneur within your company with a go-getter attitude • Analytical mind and a good hold in mathematics • Knowledge of data analysis • Strategic mindset for mitigating risk • Coordination skills to understand the requirements of different departments with regard to risk • Highly ethical • Highly motivated, well organized, detail-oriented, creative, and possess good verbal and written communication skills • Certifications of ARM, CFA, FRM, PRM is a plus • Industry experience in fintech, cross-border trade is preferred • Master degree in Engineering, Science and Mathematics is preferred
From <[https://www.10100.com/job/10201](https://www.10100.com/job/10201)>

### 5 yr modeling

<!-- OneNote page ID: {E0A1CC49-A7F4-03C4-1C9B-A0D21C7657D9}{1}{E185059447023408357961853258456561674985437} -->

The general benchmark for startups to recover CAC is 12 months or less **(1:1 ROI)**. High performing SaaS companies have an average CAC payback period of 5-7 months**(2:1 ROI).**
[https://www.geckoboard.com/best-practice/kpi-examples/cac-payback-period/#:~:text=The%20general%20benchmark%20for%20startups,have%20greater%20access%20to%20capital.](https://www.geckoboard.com/best-practice/kpi-examples/cac-payback-period/#:~:text=The%20general%20benchmark%20for%20startups,have%20greater%20access%20to%20capital.)
Original source: Matrix Partners (a $4B AUM tech focused PE) [http://www.forentrepreneurs.com/saas-metrics/?_ga=2.31076662.84312825.1730110612-340421616.1730110612](http://www.forentrepreneurs.com/saas-metrics/?_ga=2.31076662.84312825.1730110612-340421616.1730110612)
CAC Payback Period | KPI example | Geckoboard
Get a quick explanation of CAC Payback Period, including a method for calculating, and industry benchmarks. See KPI example
Sales cost = 10.5% of revenue, marketing cost = 8% of revenue, customer success = 8.5% of revenue
[https://www.saas-capital.com/blog-posts/spending-benchmarks-for-private-b2b-saas-companies/](https://www.saas-capital.com/blog-posts/spending-benchmarks-for-private-b2b-saas-companies/)

### How to pay Chinese suppliers

<!-- OneNote page ID: {E0A1CC49-A7F4-03C4-1C9B-A0D21C7657D9}{1}{E185059447023408357961853258456561674985438} -->

Wise:
you’ll bear the cost of the foreign exchange transaction. That cost includes the published fee, probably several[fixed SWIFT fees from intermediary banks](https://wise.com/au/blog/everything-you-need-to-know-about-swift-network), and the spread — the poorer exchange rate than you can find with[a currency converter online](https://wise.com/currency-converter/).
So although wire transfers may be a common method of payment, they cost the buyer much more than most realize.
From <[https://wise.com/us/blog/paying-chinese-suppliers-abroad](https://wise.com/us/blog/paying-chinese-suppliers-abroad)>

### 转型 case studies

**假发品牌 UNice 的出海之路解读，B2B外贸企业能从中学习什么？**
From <[https://www.mmldigi.com/unice-marketing-analysis/](https://www.mmldigi.com/unice-marketing-analysis/)>
"转战独立站初期，国外的玩家购买中国公司的产品都基于信用，PayPal提供了这样的信用！"
—— 小派科技CEO 任攀
"PayPal有利于把潜在买家转换成真实的买家，同时为我们商家节省了很多时间和精力。"
—— DokiDoki创始人 王舸
From <[https://www.paypal.com/c2/webapps/mpp/b2b-tranform-to-b2c](https://www.paypal.com/c2/webapps/mpp/b2b-tranform-to-b2c)>
pingpong联合创始人卢帅最近注意到了平台的一项增长数据——2024年1月1日～3月3日，既是外贸B2B收付款用户又是跨境电商（B2C）收款用户的占比增加了53个百分点。
From <[https://m.yicai.com/news/102072606.html](https://m.yicai.com/news/102072606.html)>
以北美市场为主的德威国际货运代理（上海）有限公司执行董事杨时告诉第一财经，疫情后美国国内的电商渗透率其实是有所下降的，疫情期间最高峰时曾一度接近40%，但随着美国消费的复苏，北美对跨境电商的需求总量还在增加。纵观全球，跨境电商的渗透率持续增加。随着市场蛋糕的变大，中国供应链的竞争压力让转型替代变得更加刚需，“跨境电商对传统贸易实际上具有替代作用，大量传统的中小贸易商转到了跨境电商”。
From <[https://m.yicai.com/news/102072606.html](https://m.yicai.com/news/102072606.html)>

## DTC

<!-- OneNote page ID: {E0A1CC49-A7F4-03C4-1C9B-A0D21C7657D9}{1}{E1845805134490290339618856254607888248331166} -->

根据浙商证券数据，2024年中国跨境电商独立站市场规模将达到3.4万亿元，占跨境电商B2C市场的35%。
数量方面，根据2021年7月12日国务院政策例行吹风会上公布的数据，中国独立站总数已有约20万个。据Shoplus预测，2024年中国独立站卖家数量将超过50万。
From <[https://www.cm.com/zh-cn/blog/dtc-conclusion-of-2023-and-forcast-of-2024/](https://www.cm.com/zh-cn/blog/dtc-conclusion-of-2023-and-forcast-of-2024/)>
The US DTC market has gone from being worth $128 billion in 2021, to a projection of $213 billion for the end of 2024
From <[https://www.channelsight.com/reports/direct-to-consumer-ecommerce-in-2024](https://www.channelsight.com/reports/direct-to-consumer-ecommerce-in-2024)>
中国电子商务研究中心（CECRC）公布的数据，你或许对DTC品牌出海的热度知之甚少——2018年至2022年，中国跨境独立站交易规模从1.2万亿元增长到3.6万亿元，年均增长率31.7%
From <[https://www.airwallex.com/cn/blog/dtc-payment-success-rate-payments](https://www.airwallex.com/cn/blog/dtc-payment-success-rate-payments)>
3600/7.2=500 Bn USD
Stripe = $1Tn
80% online = $800Bn
20% global market share means $4000 billion
CN penetration = 1/8
Shopify = $300Bn in 2024, at 30% mkt share =  300/30%=1,000
CN penetration = 1/2
24%year-over-year, 235B in 2023
70%[Shopify’s market share](https://2pml.com/2023/11/03/owns-dtc/)of the top 800 global DTC brands
From <[https://www.shopify.com/ie/compare/shopify-vs-woocommerce](https://www.shopify.com/ie/compare/shopify-vs-woocommerce)>
- Conservative estimates put the total Gross Merchandise Volume (GMV) from WooCommerce stores at $11.8 billion in 2019.
- Matt Mullenweg stated that, in 2020, the total sales from WooCommerce stores was more than $20 billion.
From <[https://www.mobiloud.com/blog/woocommerce-vs-shopify-market-share-statistics](https://www.mobiloud.com/blog/woocommerce-vs-shopify-market-share-statistics)>
Shopify has26.2% of the total eCommerceplatform market share, making it the most popular eCommerce platform on the internet today.
From <[https://www.mobiloud.com/blog/woocommerce-vs-shopify-market-share-statistics](https://www.mobiloud.com/blog/woocommerce-vs-shopify-market-share-statistics)>
(网经社讯)6月6日消息，在
  - [全球](http://www.100ec.cn/zt/world/)范围内，截至2024年，Shopify在独立站领域拥有26%的市场份额。在
  - [美国](http://www.100ec.cn/zt/world/)，Shopify的市场份额更加突出，达到29%
From <[https://www.100ec.cn/detail--6639426.html](https://www.100ec.cn/detail--6639426.html)>
**Shopify 73%的收入来自北美，其中56%的商家位于北美。**
Shopify给175个国家打电话，看看这些国家的商家如何以某种方式使用Shopify。**其中50%的商家来自美国，超过874K家商家。**
**欧洲、中东和非洲地区拥有43.7万卖家**，占所有商家的25%。**亚太、澳大利亚和中国地区的商家占Shopify所有商家的15%**
From <[https://www.sohu.com/a/573414877_121334945](https://www.sohu.com/a/573414877_121334945)>
根据亿邦智库发布的《2021中国DTC企业出海发展报告》，在独立站工具中，45%的企业选择Shopify，其他服务商的占比从1%~9%不等。
“去年Q3之后，Shopify在中国卖家市场的占比可能会有一些变化，我身边就有Shopify商家是最近半年转移阵地的。”Jackson指出。
From <[https://www.huxiu.com/article/425371.html](https://www.huxiu.com/article/425371.html)>
Shopify payment: $137 in 2023, 31% YoY, = 137*1.3=178.1 in 2024
[https://www.marketplacepulse.com/stats/shopify-gross-payments-volume#:~:text=The%20amount%20of%20GMV%20processed,to%20%24137.04%20billion%20in%202023](https://www.marketplacepulse.com/stats/shopify-gross-payments-volume#:~:text=The%20amount%20of%20GMV%20processed,to%20%24137.04%20billion%20in%202023).

### Stripe Paypal

<!-- OneNote page ID: {E0A1CC49-A7F4-03C4-1C9B-A0D21C7657D9}{1}{E1845805134490290339618856254607888248331170} -->

- Stripe owns68.02% of the e-commerce payment processingtechnology in the United States as of 2023.
- As of 2024, the total number of live websites that use Stripe is 1,537,487.
- As we enter into 2024, Stripe owns only a 6% share of the payment acceptance technology worldwide.
- Stripe generated $14 billion in 2022. While it was able to generate $12 billion in  2021.
- The net revenue of Stripe was $2.5 billion as of 2021.
- Stripe generated $2.255 billion in revenue in theAPACand EMEA regions as of 2022.
- Over 30% of Stripe users are from the United States.
From <[https://www.demandsage.com/stripe-statistics/](https://www.demandsage.com/stripe-statistics/)>
The following table displays the percentage of e-commerce processing platform shares owned by Stripe in different regions worldwide.
| Region | Percentage Of Share |
| --- | --- |
| Africa | 0.8% |
| APAC | 7.9% |
| EU | 15.8% |
| LATAM | 1.5% |
| North America | 70.2% |
| Others | 3.8% |
From <[https://www.demandsage.com/stripe-statistics/](https://www.demandsage.com/stripe-statistics/)>
Stripe surpassed**$1 trillion**in total payment volume (TPV) in 2023
Stripe holds 21% MSS, so global TPV is about $5 trillion
eCommerce merchants are getting 10.5% increase in revenue through higher conversion rates with Stripe’s prebuilt payment UIs.
From <[https://www.chargeflow.io/blog/stripe-statistics](https://www.chargeflow.io/blog/stripe-statistics)>
- Stripe banking-as-a-service product helps platforms create bank account replacements for customers eligible for FDIC insurance and can support ACH and wire transfers.
- Stripe Tax supports tax collection in 57 countries.
- Stripe Atlas, a service facilitating company incorporation in the U.S. (Delaware), is accessible in over 140 countries.
From <[https://www.chargeflow.io/blog/stripe-statistics](https://www.chargeflow.io/blog/stripe-statistics)>
[https://www.statista.com/statistics/895236/australia-market-share-online-payment-platforms/](https://www.statista.com/statistics/895236/australia-market-share-online-payment-platforms/)
Data obtained by Buy Shares shows that Stripe accounts for a staggering 52.84% of all e-payments online. PayPal Form is second accounting for 19.77% of all online payments. Google Pay’s 16.29% share ranks it in the third slot followed by Amazon Payments at 4.46%. Klarna has the fifth-highest online payment distribution at 3.84%. Other e-payment solutions account for the remaining 2.80%.
From <[https://focusonbusiness.eu/en/news/over-52-of-e-commerce-websites-globally-prefer-stripe-payment-processor-1/3741](https://focusonbusiness.eu/en/news/over-52-of-e-commerce-websites-globally-prefer-stripe-payment-processor-1/3741)>
- 43 million transactions were cross-border, reaching a new high of $3.2 billion.
4days = 3% of volume
“Cross-border payments drove more than 10% of total volume over the weekend, proof that Black Friday-Cyber Monday is no longer a US-centric in-person shopping day—it's a global ecommerce event,” said Emily Glassberg Sands, Stripe’s head of information. “As Black Friday-Cyber Monday becomes increasingly borderless, businesses are expanding the number of payment methods they can accept in order to reach more consumers.”
Retail giants like[Amazon](https://stripe.com/ie/newsroom/news/amazon-and-stripe),[Shopify](https://stripe.com/ie/customers/shopify),[Best Buy](https://stripe.com/ie/newsroom/news/best-buy-stripe), and[Zara](https://stripe.com/ie/newsroom/news/zara-and-stripe)turned to Stripe for its industry-leading uptime.
- Top currency volumes:  1 - US Dollar  2 - British Pound  3 - Euro  4 - Australian Dollar  5 - Canadian Dollar  6 - Japanese Yen  7 - Singapore Dollar  8 - Hong Kong Dollar  9 - New Zealand Dollar  10 - Mexican Peso

對於中國跨境商戶，本公司提供收款、付款及收單服務
商戶A是一家通過在跨境電商平台Amazon上開設的店舖銷售商品的中國商戶。
當終端買家在Amazon上訂購商戶A的產品時，本公司會幫助商戶A通過本公司提供給
商戶A的虛擬賬戶從Amazon收取美元資金（收款服務），將資金從美元或其他當地貨幣
兌換為人民幣（匯兌服務）並將兌換所得資金轉回商戶A的中國內地銀行賬戶（付款服
務）。商戶A還可利用其在虛擬賬戶上的資金向第三方支付廣告及物流服務費用（付款
服務）。與之類似，商戶B在獨立站平台Shopify上經營一家店舖。本公司幫助商戶B收
取終端買家使用主流支付方式（包括國際信用卡、本地支付、銀行卡支付及電子錢包）
支付的款項（收單服務），並將收取的這些資金轉入商戶B在本公司的虛擬賬戶（收款服
務）
於2020年、2021年、2022年及截至2023年9月30日止九個月，本公司向中國跨
境商戶提供收款、付款及收單組合服務（包括本公司僅向跨境商戶提供收款及付款服務
的情形以及本公司向跨境商戶（獨立站賣家）提供收單、收款及付款服務的情形）貢獻
的TPV分別佔本公司全球支付服務TPV的96.4%、93.6%、87.2%及78.7%
對於中國跨境企業，本公司提供收款及付款服務
企業C是一家從事國際貿易的中國企業。本公司幫助企業C收取終端買家的外幣
資金並轉至本公司提供給企業C的虛擬賬戶（收款服務），將外幣資金兌換為人民幣（匯
兌服務），並將兌換後的資金轉回企業C的中國內地銀行賬戶（付款服務）。企業C還會
利用其虛擬賬戶上的資金向第三方支付廣告及物流服務費用（付款服務）。
於2020年、2021年、2022年及截至2023年9月30日止九個月，本公司為中國跨境
企業提供收款及付款服務貢獻的TPV分別佔本公司全球支付服務TPV的3.6%、6.2%、
12.3%及19.2%
對於境內企業，本公司提供收單、收款及付款服務
企業E是一家通過其開發的微信小程序銷售商品的中國電商企業。本公司幫助企
業E向其終端買家（終端買家可以使用多種付款方式付款）收款，本公司之後將收到的
款項轉入企業E自己的境內銀行賬戶（收單服務）。在某些情形下，本公司還幫助企業E
使用收到的資金向第三方付款（付款服務）。企業E也可以選擇向本公司的備付金賬戶
充值（收款服務），之後使用這些資金進行支付（付款服務）。
於2020年、2021年、2022年及截至2023年9月30日止九個月，本公司向境內企業
提供收單、收款及付款組合服務貢獻的TPV分別佔本公司境內支付服務TPV的49.6%、
54.2%、85.1%及92.2%

根据Antom平台数据，2024年1-8月，出海中国商户数量同比增长了近90%，商户接入的支付渠道数量同比增长了约30%。中国出海商户收单交易额同比增长超过五倍，收单交易笔数同比翻三倍。其中，电商、在线旅游服务、交通出行行业的收款额增长尤为显著，这主要得益于电商独立站的快速发展、全球旅游市场的强劲恢复。
以商户数量计，电商、游戏、在线旅游服务、餐饮、在线视频与直播这五大领域的企业出海积极性最高。地域上，东南亚是近年来最热门的中国商户出海目的地之一，尤其马来西亚、印尼、泰国等地是中国出海企业最集中的市场

**Amazon**
We forecast3P GMV to grow 11.5% Y/Y and 1P to grow 5.4%in 2024, both ahead of the broader retail market
[George's Blog](https://blog.georges.blog/feed/amazons-q2-results-show-slower-retail-growth-but-continued-market-dominance)
12 Feb 2020 —Over 989,000**third-party**sellers saw a**GMV**of $220 Billion in**2019**, accounting for 59.7% of**Amazon's**total retail**GMV**of $335 Billion

Its sales have nearly doubled in just two years - from $200 billion in 2019 to $390 billion in 2021

### TPV

<!-- OneNote page ID: {E0A1CC49-A7F4-03C4-1C9B-A0D21C7657D9}{1}{E1845805134490290339618856254607888248331181} -->

鉴于商户和企业的业务发展对数字支付的需求不断增加，中国数字支付服务市场的TPV从2018年的人民币99.6万亿元增加到2022年的人民币181.2万亿元，2018年至2022年的复合年均增长率为16.1%。未来，到2027年，预计中国数字支付服务市场的TPV将增至人民币353.6万亿元，2022年至2027年的复合年均增长率为14.3%。
![research-tpv](assets/research/research-tpv/asset-001.jpg)
来源：招股书
随着商户及企业的业务持续扩张，其对增值服务的需求也持续增加。根据弗若斯特沙利文的资料，中国数字支付解决方案提供商的增值服务收入由2018年的人民币147亿元增至2022年的人民币339亿元，2018年至2022年的复合年均增长率为23.2%，预计到2027年将增至人民币883亿元，2022年至2027年的复合年均增长率为21.1%。
![research-tpv](assets/research/research-tpv/asset-002.jpg)
来源：招股书
作者：活报告
链接：https://xueqiu.com/2611594243/254827025

### China share

<!-- OneNote page ID: {E0A1CC49-A7F4-03C4-1C9B-A0D21C7657D9}{1}{E1845805134490290339618856254607888248331185} -->

[https://www.marketplacepulse.com/articles/amazons-significant-reliance-on-chinese-sellers](https://www.marketplacepulse.com/articles/amazons-significant-reliance-on-chinese-sellers)
Products from Chinese sellers represented 19 percent of Amazon's gross merchandise value in 2018. five years later, the percentage is estimated to have reached 28 percent, signaling the higher relevance of Chinese businesses on the Amazon marketplace
From <[https://www.statista.com/statistics/1469747/china-vs-non-china-based-sellers-on-amazon/](https://www.statista.com/statistics/1469747/china-vs-non-china-based-sellers-on-amazon/)>
2017年，中国卖家在亚马逊上的GMV为480亿美元，这相当于亚马逊总GMV的18%。2022年，中国卖家GMV达到了2010亿美元，涨幅为300%，占亚马逊总GMV的26%。eCommerceDB预测，到2023年底，中国卖家总的GMV将达到2380亿美元，占到亚马逊总GMV的28%
[各大电商平台争夺中国卖家](https://m.thepaper.cn/newsDetail_forward_27732185)
![research-china-share](assets/research/research-china-share/asset-002.png)
白皮书》指出，中国卖家在13个大类下都保持40％以上的市场份额，上半年销量占比最高的3个类目分别是家居与厨房、电子产品、服装&鞋子&珠宝。
From <[https://news.pingpongx.com/articles/24102111010323](https://news.pingpongx.com/articles/24102111010323)>

The top revenue bands grew faster than lower tiers, indicating successful sellers are pulling further ahead as the marketplace matures. ## 2024 market trends

<!-- OneNote page ID: {E0A1CC49-A7F4-03C4-1C9B-A0D21C7657D9}{1}{E185059447023408357961853258456561674985444} -->

<u>**半托管**</u>
SHEIN的半托管模式5月初正式上线，首发站为美国。
如果说2023年是跨境电商全托管模式风起云涌的一年，那么2024年的风头则属于半托管。
在SHEIN之前，速卖通和Temu也已先后推出半托管模式。Temu半托管上线后，平台有一定的流量倾斜，且因其快速配送和前台打标的显著优势，迅速吸引了大量订单。阿里也在财报中提到，基于全托管和半托管的Choice业务，成为了拉动速卖通订单同比增长60%的主要动力，今年1月，Choice订单已经占到平台整体订单量的一半
From <[https://m.ebrun.com/548635.html](https://m.ebrun.com/548635.html)>
2024年，Temu，SHEIN，TikTokShop，速卖通等跨境电商平台纷纷推出了半托管模式
From <[https://www.google.com/search?q=tik+tok+%E5%8D%8A%E6%89%98%E7%AE%A1&rlz=1C1GCEU_zh-CNHK1131HK1131&oq=tik+tok+%E5%8D%8A%E6%89%98%E7%AE%A1&gs_lcrp=EgZjaHJvbWUyBggAEEUYOTIJCAEQIRgKGKAB0gEINTQxMWowajSoAgCwAgE&sourceid=chrome&ie=UTF-8](https://www.google.com/search?q=tik+tok+%E5%8D%8A%E6%89%98%E7%AE%A1&rlz=1C1GCEU_zh-CNHK1131HK1131&oq=tik+tok+%E5%8D%8A%E6%89%98%E7%AE%A1&gs_lcrp=EgZjaHJvbWUyBggAEEUYOTIJCAEQIRgKGKAB0gEINTQxMWowajSoAgCwAgE&sourceid=chrome&ie=UTF-8)>
<u>**本本**</u>
- 请问入驻对公司主体有要求吗？比如要求必须是美国公司？
答：国内和美国公司主体都可以入驻。
- 提供的亚马逊资料，跟注册shein公司的资料不一样可以吗？
答：可以。
From <[https://www.tikmk.com/essay/173](https://www.tikmk.com/essay/173)>
本对本是对全托管的补充，全托管依旧是Temu跨境业务最主流、最核心的业务模式。Temu兴起于特殊时期，受益小额包裹免税政策，这才得以快速崛起。
**在Temu的拓展规划中，第一期美国，第二期欧洲，后续才是其他国家。匈牙利仅是欧洲众国中的一员，但Temu表现强劲。**
From <[https://news.qq.com/rain/a/20241024A08VVW00](https://news.qq.com/rain/a/20241024A08VVW00)>
**十三、美国**[**TikTok小店**](https://www.cifnews.com/product/8918?origin=article_word)**，到底哪种更好？**
1、本土店最好，因为功能最全，达人私信和样品功能不受限。
10月后美国ACCU店，最受追捧，但达人功能有所限制，11月起审核缓慢；
From <[https://www.cifnews.com/article/151377](https://www.cifnews.com/article/151377)>
The cap was originally $200 but it was raised by then-US president Barack Obama to $800 in February 2016. The increase was aimed at supporting local e-commerce firms such as Amazon.
But Shein and Temu have used the amended rule to expand their businesses in the US, especially after the Trump administration imposed an additional 25% tariff on many Chinese goods in 2018
From <[https://asiatimes.com/2024/09/us-seeks-to-slam-shut-shein-temu-trade-loophole/](https://asiatimes.com/2024/09/us-seeks-to-slam-shut-shein-temu-trade-loophole/)>

## CN license

<!-- OneNote page ID: {E0A1CC49-A7F4-03C4-1C9B-A0D21C7657D9}{1}{E185059447023408357961853258456561674985429} -->

Global procurement (e.g. Lianlian):
就在11月19日，[亚马逊](https://xueqiu.com/S/AMZN?from=status_stock_match)海外官方旗舰店正式在[京东](https://xueqiu.com/S/JD?from=status_stock_match)开业，11月8日亚马逊全球开店亚太区首个创新中心于深圳正式启用；这是另外一个信号，基于此和连连的布局，做跨境差旅和海外采购场景，会不会可以有更多的猜想呢？另外老鱼了解到现在已经有多个海外跨境支付企业在国内开展业务，这种市场的聚焦，如何将海外的东西卖给中国，如何把中国的产品销售至全球，通过跨境电商平台和跨境支付平台的互补，市场之大值得期待
From <[https://xueqiu.com/2772493497/313474831?md5__1038=Qq0xcD2D0D9jeGNPexUOQDgWxR0Y3aeCW4D](https://xueqiu.com/2772493497/313474831?md5__1038=Qq0xcD2D0D9jeGNPexUOQDgWxR0Y3aeCW4D)>
**跨境外汇持牌第三方支付机构跨境支付业务聚焦电商、旅游、留学三大领域**
![research-cn-license](assets/research/research-cn-license/asset-001.png)
目前，持有跨境[支付牌照](https://global.lianlianpay.com/tags/Payment_license)的全部30家企业都覆盖电商领域（货物贸易）。同时，旅游（酒店住宿、机票航空、旅游服务）和留学的聚焦度紧随其后。电商、旅游和留学已成为跨境支付业务中最重要的三个领域。
**跨境外汇牌照&****跨境人民币支付许可的区别**
- 交易背景不同：一个以外币结算，一个以人民币结算
- 业务领域不同：跨境外汇牌照只适用于货物贸易、酒店、旅游、留学等几大业务领域，而跨境人民币支付许可尚未对领域有明确要求

### Question logs

<!-- OneNote page ID: {E0A1CC49-A7F4-03C4-1C9B-A0D21C7657D9}{1}{E185059447023408357961853258456561674985431} -->

Without PBOC license, PayO:
  - Inbound:
    - Buy CNH in HK, send CNH to partners in HK, partners move funds into Mainland and disburse
    - Send USD to partners in HK, partners move funds into Mainland and converts to CNY before disburse
  - Outbound:
    - Customers send money to partners in Mainland, partners move funds to HK, convert to USD, add fund to PayO account, and PayO settles with global suppliers
With a PBOC license:
  - Inbound:
    - **DIFFERENT**: Buy CNH in HK, then PayEco replaces partners
    - **SAME**: Send USD to partners in HK - b/c PayEco does not have SAFE, cannot replace this part yet
  - Outbound
    - **DIFFE****RENT**: PayEco replaces Baofu (aggregator), saves 0.2% rebate. But PayEco needs a license for travel vertical
With a Safe license:
[question] Why we acquired PBOC license, since the partnership motion works?
[question] Only for the CNH movement, not for USD movement - USD movement remains with local partners?
[question] From YeePay: how do we provide umbrella account capabilities?

Airwallex 的创始人关于稳定币错在哪里？
  1. 85% transactions not on G10 currencies

If you look at the[IMF](https://www.elibrary.imf.org/view/journals/001/2024/085/article-A001-en.xml)or[Chainalysis](https://www.chainalysis.com/blog/2023-global-crypto-adoption-index/)data, it’s evident that crypto adoption correlates meaningfully with inflation, unstable monetary regimes, and past histories of sovereign default. Stablecoins offer a self-custodied, credible USD liability outside the banking system
From <[https://medium.com/@nic__carter/five-perspectives-on-stablecoins-5bc20076270a](https://medium.com/@nic__carter/five-perspectives-on-stablecoins-5bc20076270a)>
stablecoins largely back themselves with short term US government debt. Collectively they rank 16th among sovereign holders of the debt, and 14th among US money market mutual funds
From <[https://medium.com/@nic__carter/five-perspectives-on-stablecoins-5bc20076270a](https://medium.com/@nic__carter/five-perspectives-on-stablecoins-5bc20076270a)>
<u>**Conclusion:**</u>
Payoneer plays two roles, localization, and global money movement.
  - Stablecoin could replace PSP's role in localization, if Marketplaces and Platforms support paying sellers and creators with Stablecoin
    - Then sellers don't need a PS. They only need a crypto wallet!
    - **Then we will be disrupted**
  - But to spend Stablecoin, there's still challenge:
    - Back in China: still need to fx to CN fiat
    - Internationally: still likely require to fx to local fiat unless in Latam, etc. where already dollarized
What Payoneer could do:
  - Work with bank partners who can co-create working KYC mechanism to allow fx from stablecoin to fiat (Payoneer exchange at Tether / Circle as an institute; able to provide UD; able to use Tether / Circle as source of fund)
  - Checkout solution that supports stablecoin payment
It is equivalent to vast majority of our customers whether they hold USD or USDT in our wallet. They already have a wallet

### China views

<!-- OneNote page ID: {E0A1CC49-A7F4-03C4-1C9B-A0D21C7657D9}{1}{E1845805134490290339618856254607888248331196} -->

**第一，稳定币的核心属性究竟应界定为货币还是资产？**虽然明确区别于比特币的资产属性，更多具备货币属性，但这引出一个根本性问题：既然传统电子支付体系（如微信钱包）已能实现1：1的货币数字化（1元人民币始终等同于1元人民币价值），为何还需要额外创设稳定币？这种“叠床架屋”式的设计是否必要？
**第二，稳定币的货币属性重要还是技术属性重要？**央行数字货币（CBDC）包含数字货币（DC）和电子支付系统（EP）双层结构。使用稳定币的关键是运用其可编程智能合约技术，还是必须依赖其作为特定“币”的形态？可编程智能合约技术是否与特定“币”的形态存在必然绑定？
**第三，若稳定币的可编程智能合约技术确具优势，传统银行体系面临何种实质性技术障碍或法律障碍导致其无法采用或快速复制？**相对于非银行机构特别是非金融机构，银行机构普遍拥有显著的资金优势，是否存在维护现有盈利模式、不愿利用新技术降低用户成本并提升服务便利性的因素，抑或存在其他原因？
**第四，金融资产或实物资产的“代币化”与“证券化”之间是何种关系？**强调资产代币化服务于直接融资的模式，是否意味着完全排除金融中介服务？在此模式下，普通投资者的权益保护机制如何有效构建？
稳定币的局限性：
  1. 目前的交易规模大部分不是商业支付，而是交易所转结
  1. 银行的服务不能完全被取代，特别是非点到点的货到付款模式。还是需要escrow,信用证,项目融资等
  1. 如果技术被验证有效，银行机构亦可以采纳其能力，达到更低成本、更高效率
  1. RMB:若严格限定于境外使用的“两头在外”模式，则对内地直接影响相对有限。
From <[https://finance.sina.com.cn/money/bank/2025-06-23/doc-infazrrn9632545.shtml?cre=tianyi&mod=pchp&loc=33&r=0&rfunc=6&tj=cxvertical_pc_hp&tr=12](https://finance.sina.com.cn/money/bank/2025-06-23/doc-infazrrn9632545.shtml?cre=tianyi&mod=pchp&loc=33&r=0&rfunc=6&tj=cxvertical_pc_hp&tr=12)>
在发展路径上，“先境外离岸再境内离岸”，从香港逐步向内地自贸区推广。先支持香港发行交易离岸人民币稳定币，在汇率管理、资本流动、风险防控、用户权益保护等方面积累经验
From <[https://finance.sina.com.cn/jjxw/2025-06-11/doc-inezsutk7999941.shtml](https://finance.sina.com.cn/jjxw/2025-06-11/doc-inezsutk7999941.shtml)>

### Mbridge from Bea

<!-- OneNote page ID: {E0A1CC49-A7F4-03C4-1C9B-A0D21C7657D9}{1}{E1910089936624454777571939434546863098954571} -->

hi all! more exciting news and sharing in case you did not see yet
****
The **Financial Times** is reporting that China’s central bank is launching a digital currency-focused cross-border payment program called mBridge, in partnership with the central banks of Hong Kong, Saudi Arabia, Thailand, and the UAE, and will be a competitor to Swift. Ripple and Bitso have partnered to expand stablecoin settlement in LATAM, with Bitso's regulated Mexican peso-backed stablecoin, MXNB, to be issued on the XRP Ledger (XRPL) and integrated into Ripple's Payments on Decentralized Exchange (DEX) infrastructure. Orbital, a UK-based payments orchestration platform for both digital assets and traditional currencies with ~$12B in annualized volume, announced plans to expand into the US, following US regulatory approvals and money transmission licenses.
any local insight?
[China tees up digital payments system to compete with dollar 2.pdf](https://payoneerinc-my.sharepoint.com/personal/beaor_payoneer_com/Documents/Microsoft%20Teams%20Chat%20Files/China%20tees%20up%20digital%20payments%20system%20to%20compete%20with%20dollar%202.pdf)
The first time I saw mbridge reported on news is several years ago already actually.. I just searched a bit again the related news locally, most still comment it in a “transition stage from POC to actual use cases, with possibility to expand to more markets and huge potentials”. Some reports mentioned” there are already quite a few actual commercial cases among China, HK, Thailand and UAE” and “transaction volume is growing very fast” .. however actual number is not disclosed publicly. Some reports said estimated accumulative volume has reached over 50B USD and first half of 2025 alone could be over 40B RMB. . And so far it’s only applied to enterprise not individual yet. Looks promising and shows China’s strong determination of exploring new ways of CBT payment and challenge the US-dominant rule..
Tyler Yan might have more insights
**My read of Chinese sources: it serves centralized inter-bank clearance and does not affect our underserved SMB segments directly**
****
mBridge digital currency platform facilitates settlement between central banks, with potential future expansion to commercial banking institutions. It is important to note that the system does not support direct transactions between businesses. While the initiative cites benefits for SMBs, the primary beneficiaries remain entities already served by the traditional commercial banks.
Unlike mainstream stablecoins and cryptocurrencies, which allow for decentralized transactions, underserved SMBs cannot transact independently on this platform. They do not hold the mBridge currencies and must operate through central banks to utilize the system.
The initiative currently focuses on state-owned enterprises and bulk commodity trading, such as energy imports from Gulf nations. The strategic objective is to mitigate reliance on the SWIFT and CHIPS networks, thereby hedging against potential sanctions and geopolitical risks.

<u>**20260714 Chat with Enterprise North Lucy**</u><u>**（关于客户本地银行需求）：**</u>
1.开户和验证阶段的需求：
比如说eBay美国，我们现在只能做跨境收款，不能提供本地银行账户收款。因为本地银行账户没有代表性的“付几毛钱”的验证功能（即小额打款验证），或者说是提交的流水不符合eBay的要求。
2.线下支付需求：
如果是在美国有办公室和员工的客户，可能会需要支付房租、水电费和员工工资。
3.调研计划：
在Discovery阶段，可以去访问常征的一个客户。他是实际在用美国本地银行的，可以去了解一下他们具体需要哪些功能。
4.融资与审计需求：
之前提到过，有上市需求的客户，可能需要更标准的流水账单来做融资和审计。

**Subject:** Need your support: PS Debit Capability Request
Hi Bas and Sean,
Recently, we've received multiple requests from the frontline regarding debit capability on PS. Could you help assess whether this could be a quick win or would require significant R&D effort?
Two key use cases:
  - 1.               **OTTO Marketplace (Germany)** – OTTO is one of Germany's largest e-commerce marketplaces. They require a PS that supports both credits and debits, which can be supported by LianLian. (EUPS)
  - 2.               **Shopify Payment Refunds** – Merchants need to issue refunds to shoppers, and Shopify Payment's preferred workflow is to debit the refund amount directly from the registered bank account. Since our PS does not currently support debit transactions, this has become a blocker for some merchants when processing refunds.
As similar requests continue to increase, we'd appreciate your assessment on feasibility and potential next steps. Thanks~
From <[https://outlook.office.com/mail/inbox/id/AAkALgAAAAAAHYQDEapmEc2byACqAC%2FEWg0AudgoMjph%2BU6e3nhme9mC7wAGIsN4GQAA?nativeVersion=1.2026.630.300](https://outlook.office.com/mail/inbox/id/AAkALgAAAAAAHYQDEapmEc2byACqAC%2FEWg0AudgoMjph%2BU6e3nhme9mC7wAGIsN4GQAA?nativeVersion=1.2026.630.300)>
Hi Ivy, Michael转了这封邮件给我，产品已经在研究你描述的这些功能了，不过是从美国开始做。想问一下你的insights：
1.如果这个类似本地银行的功能(可以debit)只在美国提供,对Enterprise的客户，那还有价值吗？因为我看你举的是欧洲的例子Ivy: USPS也需要的，就是我列出来的第二个收shopify payment的那个场景。另外，我想确认一下你说的“只在美国提供”是指AH必须是美国的吗？还是说目前只有USPS可支持debit？
2.你觉得这个功能的重要性会比PS更大吗？(除了IDR、Mexico，其他的东南亚PS、欧洲PS等等都还没排上Road Map)这个功能占用的是跟PS同样的开发团队的资源。Ivy:我一般都是从客户的需求来判定重要性。说实话，目前剩下的这些PS的紧迫性没有那么高了。但是debit PS我还是一直有听到一些ask的。如果他们能够短期快速实现，我认为值得调整一下目前的优先级。
From <[https://outlook.office.com/mail/inbox/id/AAkALgAAAAAAHYQDEapmEc2byACqAC%2FEWg0AudgoMjph%2BU6e3nhme9mC7wAGIsN4JQAA?nativeVersion=1.2026.630.300](https://outlook.office.com/mail/inbox/id/AAkALgAAAAAAHYQDEapmEc2byACqAC%2FEWg0AudgoMjph%2BU6e3nhme9mC7wAGIsN4JQAA?nativeVersion=1.2026.630.300)>

### FBA check-in with Nana

<!-- OneNote page ID: {E0A1CC49-A7F4-03C4-1C9B-A0D21C7657D9}{1}{E19481503317730160476020141735084548107506391} -->

3 Shopify customers inquired about debit payment feature to top up negative balance:
  1. Shopify payment uses Stripe solution; sometimes give them a few days ddl to top up balance, otherwise may close their channel
  1. Hard to reach Shopify payment customer service, and they don't provide resolution
  1. So customers comes to Payo for help… at time we don't have a solution for them (Shopify does not take PON or credit card
Not a requirement for our own CKO customers. We back up our customers - we are willing to use customer's payoneer balance to top up Stripe balance.
If we provide this feature:
  1. On the one hand, we can prevent some loss of revenue. For one customer inquired, they used US bank account unwillingly to top up, leaving a trace of business connection between accounts and showed up transactions in otherwise clean US bank account. Although switch back to Payoneer PS to receive from shopify after a month, lost revenue in that month
    1. Customer claimed that WF provides this, but turn out not accurate
    1. However, if competitors do this, we risk churning our PSP withdrawal customers
  1. On the other hand, if we proactively solve this issue for customer, they may never have an incentive to adopt us for checkout. They chose Shopify for a reason (cost or higher conversion).
    1. We may still have a chance to get their BNPL volume, where we are cost competitive
Verdict: not a critical feature, and we won't market publically (likely no monetization). Only for VIP customers for both CKO and PSP Withdrawal to increase stickiness.
